raw=(pos-last.pos)/ddt local clamped=Vector3.new(math.clamp(raw.X,-150,150),math.clamp(raw.Y,-150,150),math.clamp(raw.Z,-150,150))if rec.ready then rec.vel=rec.vel:Lerp(clamped,0.5)else rec.vel=clamped rec.ready=true end end end table.insert(rec.s,{pos=pos,t=now})while#rec.s>VIS_SAMPLES do table.remove(rec.s,1)end return rec.vel end local function visReadVelocity(tgt,now)local raw=tgt.AssemblyLinearVelocity local tracked=visTrueVelocity(tgt,now)if not tracked or tracked.Magnitude<0.5 then if raw.Magnitude>400 then return Vector3.zero end return raw end local diff=(raw-tracked).Magnitude local rawMag,trackMag=raw.Magnitude,tracked.Magnitude if rawMag>250 then return tracked end if diff>math.max(80,trackMag*1.5)then return tracked end return raw end local function stepVisibleBypass(root,hum,target)local me=root.Position local them=target.Position local spd=tonumber(_G._VezyBypassChaseSpeed)or aimbotSpeed()local now=os.clock()if now-VIS.lastClean>=5 then VIS.lastClean=now for r in pairs(VIS.track)do if not r.Parent then VIS.track[r]=nil end end end local targetVel=visReadVelocity(target,now)local predictPos=them+targetVel*0.14+target.CFrame.LookVector*0.3 local direction=predictPos-me local flat=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)local flatDir=flat.Magnitude>0.01 and flat.Unit or Vector3.zero local yVel=(them.Y+3.7-me.Y)*19.5+targetVel.Y*0.8 if hum.FloorMaterial~=Enum.Material.Air then yVel=math.max(yVel,13)end yVel=math.clamp(yVel,-70,110)local desiredVel=Vector3.new(flatDir.X*spd,yVel,flatDir.Z*spd)root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel,0.8)VIS.intended=desiredVel local predictTime=math.clamp(targetVel.Magnitude/150,0.05,0.2)local predictedPos=them+targetVel*predictTime if(predictedPos-me).Magnitude>0.1 then local rx,ry,rz=(root.CFrame:Inverse()*CFrame.lookAt(me,predictedPos)):ToEulerAnglesXYZ()root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(math.clamp(rx,-2.5,2.5)*42,math.clamp(ry,-2.5,2.5)*42,math.clamp(rz,-2.5,2.5)*42))end local v=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity if math.abs(v.X)>350 or math.abs(v.Z)>350 then root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=VIS.intended end return(root.Position-them).Magnitude<=8 end local EXO={cfg={ACTIVATE_DISTANCE=13,MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE=1,PREDICTION_TIME=0.22,PREDICT_AHEAD=3,MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE=150,VELOCITY_SMOOTHING=0.2,MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY=80,SERVER_TICKRATE=1/60,ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT=0.3,DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME=0.12,QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER=1.5,GRAVITY=196.2,AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR=0.8,MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME=0.08,MIN_PING_COMPENSATION=0.04,MAX_PING_COMPENSATION=0.35,},st={},currentPing=0.1,}local function exoAvg(list)if#list==0 then return Vector3.zero end local sum=Vector3.zero for _,v in ipairs(list)do sum=sum+v end return sum/#list end local function exoPush(list,value,maxSize)table.insert(list,value)if#list>maxSize then table.remove(list,1)end end local function exoReset()EXO.st={target=nil,lastTargetPos=nil,targetVelocity=Vector3.zero,smoothedVelocity=Vector3.zero,accelerationHistory={},aerialVelocityHistory={},previousDirection=nil,lastDirectionChangeTime=0,airborneTime=0,lastSwing=EXO.st and EXO.st.lastSwing or 0,}end exoReset()local function exoRefreshPing()local ms=100 pcall(function()local stats=game:GetService("\083\116\097\116\115")local net=stats and stats.Network local item=net and net.ServerStatsItem local pingItem=item and item["\068\097\116\097\032\080\105\110\103"]if pingItem and pingItem.GetValue then ms=pingItem:GetValue()end end)if type(ms)~="\110\117\109\098\101\114"then ms=100 end EXO.currentPing=math.clamp(ms/1000,EXO.cfg.MIN_PING_COMPENSATION,EXO.cfg.MAX_PING_COMPENSATION)end local function exoFace(root,direction)if direction.Magnitude<0.01 then return end local cross=root.CFrame.LookVector:Cross(direction.Unit)local angle=math.asin(math.clamp(cross.Magnitude,-1,1))root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=cross.Magnitude>0.01 and cross.Unit*angle*80 or Vector3.zero end local function stepExoticV3(root,target,targetHum,dt)local C,S=EXO.cfg,EXO.st if S.target~=target then exoReset();S=EXO.st;S.target=target end local now=os.clock()local targetPos=target.Position local dtc=math.max(dt or 1/60,1/240)if S.lastTargetPos then local instantVel=(targetPos-S.lastTargetPos)/dtc local accel=instantVel-S.targetVelocity if accel.Magnitude>C.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE then instantVel=S.targetVelocity+accel.Unit*C.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE end local hv=Vector3.new(instantVel.X,0,instantVel.Z)if hv.Magnitude>C.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY then hv=hv.Unit*C.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY instantVel=Vector3.new(hv.X,instantVel.Y,hv.Z)end exoPush(S.accelerationHistory,instantVel-S.targetVelocity,8)S.targetVelocity=instantVel S.smoothedVelocity=S.smoothedVelocity:Lerp(instantVel,C.VELOCITY_SMOOTHING)end S.lastTargetPos=targetPos local isAirborne=targetHum and targetHum.FloorMaterial==Enum.Material.Air S.airborneTime=isAirborne and(S.airborneTime+dtc)or 0 if isAirborne and S.airborneTime>=C.MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME then exoPush(S.aerialVelocityHistory,S.targetVelocity,6)elseif not isAirborne then S.aerialVelocityHistory={}end local smoothedVel=S.smoothedVelocity if isAirborne and#S.aerialVelocityHistory>0 then local avgAerial=exoAvg(S.aerialVelocityHistory)smoothedVel=Vector3.new(avgAerial.X,S.targetVelocity.Y,avgAerial.Z)*C.AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR end local isQuickTurn=false local htv=Vector3.new(S.targetVelocity.X,0,S.targetVelocity.Z)if htv.Magnitude>5 then local newDir=htv.Unit if S.previousDirection and S.previousDirection:Dot(newDir)<0.5 then isQuickTurn=(now-S.lastDirectionChangeTime)<C.DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME S.lastDirectionChangeTime=now end S.previousDirection=newDir end exoRefreshPing()local serverDelay=EXO.currentPing+C.SERVER_TICKRATE local adjustedDelay=isQuickTurn and(serverDelay*C.QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER)or serverDelay local avgAccel=exoAvg(S.accelerationHistory)local accelContribution=avgAccel*C.ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT*(adjustedDelay*adjustedDelay*0.5)local predictedPos=targetPos+smoothedVel*adjustedDelay+accelContribution local predictionTime=C.PREDICTION_TIME*1.1 if isAirborne then predictedPos=predictedPos+smoothedVel*predictionTime+Vector3.new(0,-0.5*C.GRAVITY*predictionTime*predictionTime,0)else predictedPos=predictedPos+smoothedVel*predictionTime end local hs=Vector3.new(smoothedVel.X,0,smoothedVel.Z)if hs.Magnitude>1 then predictedPos=predictedPos+hs.Unit*C.PREDICT_AHEAD end local direction=predictedPos-root.Position exoFace(root,direction)if direction.Magnitude>C.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE then root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=direction.Unit*aimbotSpeed()else root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(0,root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y*0.5,0)end end V2.V11={target=nil,lastTargetPos=nil,targetVelocity=Vector3.zero,smoothedVelocity=Vector3.zero,velocityHistory={},accelerationHistory={},aerialVelocityHistory={},verticalVelocityHistory={},previousDirection=nil,lastDirectionChangeTime=0,airborneTime=0,lastActivationTime=0,currentPing=0.1,realPingMs=100,lastPingRefresh=0,underSince=nil,lastRelock=0,}V2.V11.cfg={ACTIVATE_DISTANCE=13,MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE=1,PREDICTION_TIME=0.22,PREDICT_AHEAD=3,MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE=150,VELOCITY_SMOOTHING=0.2,MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY=80,SERVER_TICKRATE=1/60,MIN_PING_COMPENSATION=0.03,MAX_PING_COMPENSATION=0.25,ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT=0.3,DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME=0.12,QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER=1.5,GRAVITY=196.2,AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR=0.8,MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME=0.08,}function V2.V11.average(list)if#list==0 then return Vector3.zero end local total=Vector3.zero for _,value in ipairs(list)do total+=value end return total/#list end function V2.V11.push(list,value,maxSize)table.insert(list,value)if#list>maxSize then table.remove(list,1)end end function V2.V11.reset()V2.V11.target=nil V2.V11.lastTargetPos=nil V2.V11.targetVelocity=Vector3.zero V2.V11.smoothedVelocity=Vector3.zero V2.V11.velocityHistory={}V2.V11.accelerationHistory={}V2.V11.aerialVelocityHistory={}V2.V11.verticalVelocityHistory={}V2.V11.previousDirection=nil V2.V11.lastDirectionChangeTime=0 V2.V11.airborneTime=0 V2.V11.underSince=nil V2.V11.lastActivationTime=0 V2.V11.lastRelock=0 V2.V11.lastPingRefresh=0 end function V2.V11.refreshPing()local now=tick()if now-V2.V11.lastPingRefresh<0.5 then return end V2.V11.lastPingRefresh=now local ok,value=pcall(function()return game:GetService("\083\116\097\116\115").Network.ServerStatsItem["\068\097\116\097\032\080\105\110\103"]:GetValue()end)if ok and type(value)=="\110\117\109\098\101\114"then V2.V11.realPingMs=math.floor(value)end V2.V11.currentPing=math.clamp(V2.V11.realPingMs/1000,V2.V11.cfg.MIN_PING_COMPENSATION,V2.V11.cfg.MAX_PING_COMPENSATION)end function V2.V11.step(root,target,targetHum,dt)local C=V2.V11.cfg if V2.V11.target~=target then V2.V11.reset()V2.V11.target=target end local now=tick()local targetPos=target.Position local frameDt=math.max(dt or(1/60),1/240)if V2.V11.lastTargetPos then local rawVelocity=(targetPos-V2.V11.lastTargetPos)/frameDt local velocityDelta=rawVelocity-V2.V11.targetVelocity if velocityDelta.Magnitude>C.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE then rawVelocity=V2.V11.targetVelocity+velocityDelta.Unit*C.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE end local horizontal=Vector3.new(rawVelocity.X,0,rawVelocity.Z)if horizontal.Magnitude>C.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY then horizontal=horizontal.Unit*C.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY rawVelocity=Vector3.new(horizontal.X,rawVelocity.Y,horizontal.Z)end V2.V11.push(V2.V11.accelerationHistory,(rawVelocity-V2.V11.targetVelocity)/frameDt,4)V2.V11.push(V2.V11.velocityHistory,rawVelocity,8)V2.V11.targetVelocity=rawVelocity V2.V11.smoothedVelocity=V2.V11.smoothedVelocity:Lerp(rawVelocity,C.VELOCITY_SMOOTHING)end V2.V11.lastTargetPos=targetPos local airborne=targetHum and targetHum.FloorMaterial==Enum.Material.Air V2.V11.airborneTime=airborne and(V2.V11.airborneTime+frameDt)or 0 if airborne and V2.V11.airborneTime>=C.MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME then V2.V11.push(V2.V11.aerialVelocityHistory,V2.V11.targetVelocity,6)elseif not airborne then V2.V11.aerialVelocityHistory={}end local predictionVelocity=V2.V11.smoothedVelocity if airborne and#V2.V11.aerialVelocityHistory>0 then local averageAir=V2.V11.average(V2.V11.aerialVelocityHistory)predictionVelocity=Vector3.new(averageAir.X,V2.V11.targetVelocity.Y,averageAir.Z)*C.AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR end local quickTurn=false local horizontalVelocity=Vector3.new(V2.V11.targetVelocity.X,0,V2.V11.targetVelocity.Z)if horizontalVelocity.Magnitude>5 then local direction=horizontalVelocity.Unit if V2.V11.previousDirection and V2.V11.previousDirection:Dot(direction)<0.5 then quickTurn=(now-V2.V11.lastDirectionChangeTime)<C.DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME V2.V11.lastDirectionChangeTime=now end V2.V11.previousDirection=direction end local serverDelay=V2.V11.currentPing+C.SERVER_TICKRATE if quickTurn then serverDelay=serverDelay*C.QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER end local averageAcceleration=V2.V11.average(V2.V11.accelerationHistory)local predicted=targetPos+predictionVelocity*serverDelay+averageAcceleration*C.ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT*(serverDelay*serverDelay*0.5)local predictionTime=C.PREDICTION_TIME*1.1 if airborne then predicted=predicted+predictionVelocity*predictionTime+Vector3.new(0,-0.5*C.GRAVITY*predictionTime*predictionTime,0)else predicted=predicted+predictionVelocity*predictionTime end local flatPrediction=Vector3.new(predictionVelocity.X,0,predictionVelocity.Z)if flatPrediction.Magnitude>1 then predicted=predicted+flatPrediction.Unit*C.PREDICT_AHEAD end local toTarget=predicted-root.Position exoFace(root,toTarget)if toTarget.Magnitude>C.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE then local followSpeed=math.clamp(aimbotSpeed(),1,500)root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=toTarget.Unit*followSpeed else root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(0,root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y*0.5,0)end end local
